### [20] [ToolMinimize: Auditing and Rewriting LLM Agent Tool Calls to Minimize Privacy Exposure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24957)
*Wenbiao Li,Yuqiao Xu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文提出一种面向LLM工具调用的中间件，通过在调用前重写参数，仅保留工具执行所必需的数据，从而显著降低隐私敏感数据的过度传递，同时保持任务有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM代理常会在工具调用参数中携带超出工具需求的隐私敏感数据，并在每次调用时跨越信任边界传递给第三方服务。实验显示，即使加入明确的隐私提示，仍存在较高比例的不必要数据暴露。现有方法主要支持调用拦截或信息流标记，无法细粒度修改参数值；传统PII检测也难以识别由实体语义隐含的敏感信息。

Method: 系统作为工具调用中间件拦截参数，基于工具模式（schema）分析各字段对功能的必要性，并采用四类重写操作：删除不必要字段、对值进行泛化、用等价占位内容替换，以及截断冗余内容。对于虽属必要字段但包含自由文本的情况，可选的LLM内容必要性层进一步移除与任务无关的敏感片段。该方法不依赖所有模式都显式提供“最小必要”元数据。

Result: 在三种生产级LLM的307次真实工具调用上，系统将隐私代价降低81.2%至92.0%，且参数级任务有效性达到100%；等价性检验结果为p<0.001、Δ=1.0。在25个未标注的MCP模式上，隐私代价降低79.0%。启用可选的自由文本内容必要性层后，真实LLM调用的降幅提升至85.1%至95.6%，作者构造模式上的降幅由71.1%提升至90.9%。中位额外延迟为1.77毫秒。

Conclusion: 该工作表明，隐私保护不应仅依赖阻断调用、提示约束或PII识别，而应在工具调用边界实施模式感知的参数最小化重写。其优势在于低延迟、保持工具功能并能处理部分隐式敏感信息；主要适用前提是系统能够获取并正确解释工具模式，且自由文本场景的进一步净化依赖额外的内容必要性判断。

Abstract: LLM agents routinely include privacy-sensitive data (PSD) in tool call arguments beyond what the invoked tools require, crossing trust boundaries to third-party services on every invocation. A controlled measurement on three production LLMs (GPT-4o, Claude 3.5 Sonnet, Llama-3.3-70B) shows that 81--88\% of tool calls include unnecessary PSD under default prompts; explicit privacy instructions still leave 36--76\% over-sharing. Existing defenses gate calls (allow/block) or label flows (information-flow control) but cannot \emph{rewrite} argument values, and PII detection tools miss implicit PSD like ``Memorial Sloan Kettering'' (a hospital name that implies a diagnosis). We present \system{}, a middleware that intercepts tool calls and rewrites their arguments to the minimum data necessary for tool functionality, combining schema-aware necessity analysis with four operations: removal, generalization, substitution, and truncation. Live validation on 307 tool calls across the three LLMs above reduces privacy cost by 81.2--92.0\% at 100\% argument-level task validity (TOST equivalence $p{<}0.001$ at $Δ{=}1.0$); on 25 unannotated Model Context Protocol (MCP) schemas, by 79.0\% with no \texttt{minimum\_necessary} metadata. An optional LLM content-necessity layer strips task-irrelevant PSD from otherwise-necessary free-text fields, raising live-LLM reduction to 85.1--95.6\% and author-schema reduction from 71.1\% to 90.9\%. Median latency is 1.77\,ms.

</details>


### [21] [Retrieved But Not Reliable: A Survey on Attacks, and Defenses in Retrieval-Augmented Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24977)
*Minh Tran,Cuong Dang,Tuc Nguyen,Khanh-Tung Tran,Minh Huynh Nguyen,Trinh Chau,Kien Le,Do Xuan Long,Jiahao Zhang,Hoang D. Nguyen,Thanh Le,Suhang Wang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文是一篇面向检索增强生成（RAG）鲁棒性的综述，构建覆盖完整RAG流水线的统一分析框架，系统梳理攻击目标、威胁模型、防御策略、评测基准与可解释性方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RAG通过外部知识检索提升语言模型的事实准确性并缓解幻觉，但其检索—生成链路也引入语料污染、后门、隐私泄露和公平性受损等风险。现有综述对攻击者目标、跨组件威胁模型及分阶段防御的覆盖不足，缺少统一的流水线视角。

Method: 文章将RAG风险建模为涉及语料库、检索器和生成器的威胁模型；按攻击目标将攻击归纳为准确性、隐私和公平性三类；按流水线阶段梳理防御，包括检索、重排序、生成和溯源阶段；同时汇总鲁棒性基准与可解释性技术，用于评估和分析系统行为。

Result: 该综述形成了一个以流水线为中心的RAG鲁棒性分类体系，明确了不同组件、攻击目标与对应防御位置之间的关系，并整合了评测基准和解释方法，为比较现有研究及发现防护缺口提供结构化参考。

Conclusion: RAG鲁棒性不能仅依赖单一模块的加固，而应采用覆盖语料、检索、重排序、生成与溯源环节的端到端防护和评测框架。统一的威胁建模、分阶段防御及可解释评估是提升RAG可靠性与可信性的关键。

Abstract: Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) enhances large language models by grounding outputs in external knowledge, improving factuality and reducing hallucinations. At the same time, the retrieval-augmented pipeline introduces new robustness and security risks, including corpus poisoning, backdoor attacks, privacy leakage, and fairness violations. Despite rapid progress in this area, existing surveys remain limited in their treatment of attacker objectives, threat models, and stage-specific defenses across the full RAG pipeline. This survey presents a unified and pipeline-aware overview of RAG robustness. We formalize threat models over the corpus, retriever, and generator, and organize attacks into three main objectives: accuracy, privacy, and fairness. We further review defenses from a pipeline-aware perspective, covering the retrieval, rerank, generation, and traceback stages. In addition, we summarize robustness benchmarks and explainability methods for more deeply evaluating and explaining RAG robustness.

</details>


### [22] [Static Detection of Post-Quantum Cryptographic Algorithms in Stripped Binaries for Digital Forensic Examination and Migration Assurance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25122)
*Muhammad Shaheer Bin Junaid*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文提出Kestrel，一种面向已剥离、静态链接及优化后二进制文件的静态分析方法。该方法通过识别ML-KEM和ML-DSA实现所依赖的数论变换常量表，验证二进制中是否真实包含标准化后量子密码算法，而不依赖符号、库依赖或运行时行为等易被构建过程破坏的线索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有密码算法发现工具主要依赖符号信息、动态库关系或运行时特征；这些信息在符号剥离、静态链接、编译优化及混淆后可能消失。因此，软件供应链审计与密码迁移验证缺乏一种能直接从发布二进制中确认传统算法是否已被认可的后量子方案替代的方法。

Method: Kestrel从ML-KEM和ML-DSA公开参数推导其数论变换常量表指纹，在二进制只读数据区域中搜索相应特征。为适应编译与布局变化，方法采用归一化与多重集合匹配来定位常量表，并对误报概率进行解析性推导。该方案同时将检出结果归因至具体实现代码谱系，并可用于磁盘镜像中残留数据的检验。

Result: 在四条相互独立的实现谱系及多种构建变换条件下，包括编译器级混淆，Kestrel实现了128/128的召回率且无误报。对生产Linux系统中6,224个二进制文件的扫描发现了12个未被目录记录但包含ML-KEM的程序，涉及OpenSSH密钥交换组件及容器管理软件栈。实验还表明，部分后量子代码经语言运行时进入生产环境，分发项目本身并不知情；在磁盘镜像试验中，方法还从无法重构的已删除二进制残留区域恢复出有效检出。

Conclusion: Kestrel证明了以算法关键常量表为锚点的二进制静态识别可在缺失传统元数据时可靠确认ML-KEM与ML-DSA实现。该方法可用于密码迁移合规核验、软件供应链检查、实现来源归因及后量子密码相关的取证分析，并能区分真实代码实现与缺乏底层代码支撑的声明。

Abstract: Currently, there is no method to verify from compiled binary code whether a quantum-vulnerable algorithm has been replaced by an approved post-quantum algorithm. Cryptographic discovery tools identify algorithms by symbols, library dependencies, and runtime behaviour; however, all these signals are destroyed by stripping, statically linking, and optimising a binary. This paper presents Kestrel, a static analysis method for identifying the standardised lattice-based schemes ML-KEM and ML-DSA in stripped binary code. Kestrel identifies ML-KEM and ML-DSA by detecting the number-theoretic transform constant tables that form the read-only data upon which the arithmetic depends. The fingerprints Kestrel derives from public scheme parameters are localised by means of a normalisation-and-multiset-matching procedure; the false-positive probability is established analytically. In experiments on four independent implementation lineages and all build transformations, including compiler-level obfuscation, Kestrel achieved recall of 128 of 128 with zero false positives. Applying Kestrel to 6,224 binaries on a production Linux system disclosed twelve uncatalogued programs containing ML-KEM; these included the OpenSSH key-exchange program and the container-management stack. In several of these programs, post-quantum code entered production through the language runtime without the awareness of the projects distributing them. Kestrel distinguishes genuine post-quantum implementations from advertised claims not backed by the underlying code, attributes each detection to its originating codebase, and, in a forensic disk-image trial, recovered a detection from unallocated space after the deleted binary could no longer be reconstructed. Thus, Kestrel provides a practical basis for cryptographic migration assurance, software supply-chain inspection, and post-quantum forensic examination.

</details>


### [23] [CA-less Mutual Co-Signing of Documents over a Unidirectional Visual Channel with Transported Hardware Attestation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25144)
*Dmytro Diikun*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文提出一种面向两台移动设备的离线互签协议：设备仅通过单向、易丢失、低带宽的光学动画码通信，不依赖中介服务器或证书机构；协议利用安全元件证明与两阶段哈希锚点，实现可离线验证的双签名文档绑定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有移动端联合签署通常依赖网络服务、证书机构或稳定的双向通信，难以适用于受限连接场景；同时，交互式互签存在“首个签名时尚未知第二方身份”的循环依赖。论文旨在以硬件证明替代传统证书信任链，并在视觉单向传输条件下解决身份与文档的完整绑定问题。

Method: 协议以屏幕动画码向对方摄像头单向传输数据，并用LT类喷泉码提高丢包环境下大尺寸硬件证明令牌的传输可靠性。信任根来自平台安全元件生成的证明令牌，令牌经密码学方式纳入联合签名。核心设计为两阶段哈希锚点：第一方先对文档建立不含第二方身份信息的承诺锚点；随后将第二方身份绑定至该承诺形成后续锚点，从而避免重签第一方签名。论文定义锚点绑定、联合签名不可分离性、证明绑定的密钥来源与签后篡改可检测四项安全性质，并在哈希抗碰撞、签名EUF-CMA安全及安全元件理想签名预言机模型下给出归约。

Result: 作者实现了iOS/Android原型，采用Secure Enclave与StrongBox中的ECDSA P-256、SHA-256、Apple App Attest与Play Integrity，以及LT风格喷泉码。独立第三方验证器可在完全离线状态下重算全部锚点，并验证双方签名。摘要表明该方案在工程上可部署，且能满足其定义的四类安全目标；但摘要未给出传输时延、丢包率下的恢复性能、证明令牌尺寸、跨平台互操作成功率和用户交互成本等量化数据。

Conclusion: 该工作将硬件密钥证明、喷泉编码视觉传输与两阶段哈希承诺结合，针对无服务器、无证书、单向低带宽通信下的移动端互签提出了结构清晰的解决方案。其主要学术价值在于消除交互互签中的循环签名依赖，并提供离线可验证的端到端信任绑定。后续应重点评估真实环境下的性能、平台证明接口的长期可用性与撤销机制、设备失陷或证明服务异常时的安全边界，以及协议对用户确认界面的依赖。

Abstract: We describe and analyze a protocol for mutual co-signing of a document by two mobile devices that (i) communicate only over a one-way, lossy, low-bandwidth optical channel (an animated on-screen code read by the counterparty's camera), (ii) use no intermediary server on the trust path, and (iii) use no certificate authority. Trust in each party's public key is instead grounded in a hardware attestation token produced by the platform secure element, transported in full over the visual channel by a rateless (fountain) code and cryptographically bound into the co-signature. The core technical contribution is a two-stage hash anchor that removes the circular signing dependency inherent to interactive co-signing: the first party commits to the document before the identity of the second party is known, and the second party's identity is later bound to that commitment without invalidating the first signature. We give a threat model, define four security properties (anchor binding, co-signature inseparability, attestation-bound key provenance, and post-signing tamper evidence) and reduce them to standard assumptions (collision resistance of H and EUF-CMA security of the underlying signature scheme), with the secure element modeled as an ideal signing oracle. We report a working instantiation on iOS/Android using ECDSA P-256 in the Secure Enclave/StrongBox, SHA-256, Apple App Attest / Play Integrity, and an LT-style fountain code, together with an independent third-party verifier that recomputes all anchors and checks both signatures fully offline.

</details>


### [24] [BGPay: An Incentive-Compatible Mechanism for BGP Hijack Filtering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25165)
*Tomasz Sadowy,Constantine Doumanidis,Maria Apostolaki*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文提出 BGPay：通过悬赏与托管机制激励网络运营者过滤无效路由公告，并利用公共路由采集器作为独立证据源自动结算奖励。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 RPKI/ROV 等防护存在激励错配：具备过滤能力的网络承担部署和运营成本，而前缀所有者获得主要收益，导致过滤意愿不足。

Method: 前缀所有者为其前缀设置持续性悬赏；过滤者与监测方在信息披露前先提交承诺；智能合约依据公共路由采集器中可验证的路由表证据进行结算，而非依赖前缀所有者主观裁定。奖励额度按过滤行动对异常路由传播遏制的影响确定。

Result: 对 1,000 起真实事件的分析显示，公共路由采集器在关键位置具有足够可见性；对遏制传播更重要的自治系统通常也更容易被公共监测点观测，因此可基于可验证证据实施与影响相称的奖励。

Conclusion: BGPay 将路由过滤从缺乏直接回报的公共行为转化为可验证、可结算的市场化交易。其核心可行性依赖于公共监测基础设施的覆盖度，以及按遏制影响设计奖励以降低参与者不当行为的动机。

Abstract: BGP hijacking remains a persistent threat as existing defenses, including RPKI/ROV suffer from a fundamental incentive misalignment: the networks best positioned to filter malicious announcements bear operational costs but receive no direct benefit, while the victim prefix owner captures all the value. We advocate a market-based alternative in which prefix owners post standing bounties for filtering invalid announcements of their prefixes, turning filtering from altruism into a private transaction. Our insight is that neither a propagating hijack nor its absence can hide from public route collectors, whose committed routing tables could become an independent root of trust for releasing funds of the bounty. We build on this insight to design BGPay, an escrow protocol in which filterers and monitors commit before either reveals, and a smart contract pays out on evidence rather than on the prefix owner's judgment. Analyzing 1K real hijack incidents, we find that today's collectors already provide enough visibility where it matters: ASes that are more important for containing the hijack are also highly visible from the public monitors. Hence, setting rewards proportionately to containment impact discourages misbehavior.

</details>


### [25] [Authenticated Data Structures for Dynamic Workloads](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25206)
*Ziheng Shangguan,Aviv Yaish,Dahlia Malkhi*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文提出霍夫曼-默克尔树（HMT），一种面向访问频率动态变化工作负载的认证数据结构。通过频率感知的树布局与弹性冷热分层，HMT在真实数据实验中同时降低更新哈希开销和按访问频率加权的成员证明长度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有认证数据结构通常未充分处理访问偏斜及其随时间变化的问题：高频与低频项目若采用统一、固定的树布局，会使高频访问承担不必要的深度与证明成本。论文旨在为大规模、可变状态提供能够随访问模式调整的高效认证索引。

Method: HMT包含两项核心设计：（1）基于霍夫曼编码的默克尔树布局，使高频项目更接近根节点，并扩展该布局以支持访问频率演化；（2）弹性分层机制，将项目划分至热、冷等独立树中，并通过自适应迁移调整层级。为控制动态维护成本，系统以批处理方式更新布局，使用Count-Min Sketch估计访问频率，设置层级提升缓存，并比较多种跨层迁移策略。实验将HMT与Merkle Patricia Trie（MPT）和Unified Binary Tree（UBT）比较，指标为每次更新的哈希计算量及访问频率加权的成员证明大小。

Result: 最佳HMT策略相较MPT，平均哈希操作量约为其2.4倍更少，即约降至0.42倍；相较UBT约降至0.34倍。其成员证明长度相较MPT降至0.18倍，相较UBT降至0.55倍。结果表明，频率感知布局尤其能显著压缩高频访问所对应的证明成本。

Conclusion: HMT证明了将访问频率纳入认证数据结构布局优化具有实际价值。霍夫曼式深度分配与动态冷热分层能够在百万级项目、GB级数据规模下有效适应访问模式变化，并在更新计算成本和加权证明成本之间取得优于MPT与UBT的综合性能。

Abstract: We introduce the Huffman-Merkle Tree (HMT), an authenticated data structure (ADS) for dynamic workloads where items may differ in access frequencies, and access frequencies can change over time. An ADS allows proving item membership against a short commitment to a large mutable state, with applications including verifiable storage, Internet transparency services, and blockchains. Optimizing ADS performance under continuously changing access frequencies has not been fully addressed before, neither in theory nor in practice. HMT addresses dynamically changing access skew through two complementary mechanisms. The first is a Huffman-coding-based Merkle-tree layout, with a novel extension to support evolving access frequencies. The second is an elastic tiering regime that partitions items across separate trees, such as hot and cold tiers, with adaptive migration between them. The key insight in this approach is to place frequently accessed items closer to the root, while assigning less frequently accessed items to progressively larger and deeper trees. This reduces the overall frequency-weighted access cost. Our scheme is designed to scale to gigabytes of data spanning millions of items. To handle dynamism efficiently, layout updates are applied in batches, access frequencies are tracked using a count-min sketch, and the system employs a tier-promotion cache while exploring multiple tier-migration policies. We implement HMT and compare it on real-world data with Ethereum's Merkle Patricia Trie (MPT) ADS and its proposed replacement, the Unified Binary Tree (UBT). Our evaluation considers two metrics: the amount of hashing per update and access-weighted membership-proof size. The latter captures both item access cost and frequency. We find that the best HMT policy uses about 2.4x and 0.34x less average hash operations than MPT and UBT respectively, and has 0.18x and 0.55x shorter proofs.

</details>


### [26] [Automotive HSMs - Architectural Challenges and Security Implications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25216)
*Krishna Teja Medam,Austin Bruce*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文分析汽车硬件安全模块（HSM）的体系结构及其在安全启动与硬件信任根中的作用，系统讨论集成模式、威胁模型、安全机制、设计权衡和未来挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 汽车电子控制单元日益依赖硬件级安全来保护软件完整性、真实性和生命周期管理，同时还需满足实时性、成本、远程攻击与物理攻击防护等要求，因此有必要评估HSM的架构选择及其安全影响。

Method: 论文调研量产ECU中的典型HSM集成模式及应用场景，构建现实威胁模型，并从安全启动信任链、安全存储、安全执行和软件签名等方面分析HSM设计；同时讨论隔离性、性能、可更新性、攻击面及侧信道风险之间的权衡。

Result: 研究表明，HSM能够通过隔离的密码服务、安全密钥存储和受控执行支持汽车SoC建立硬件信任根与安全启动链。不同集成架构在安全隔离、运行性能、升级灵活性和攻击面方面存在明显取舍，并面临规模化部署、系统韧性、密码敏捷性及后量子密码适配等挑战。

Conclusion: 汽车HSM是构建可信ECU和安全启动体系的关键基础设施。未来架构应在安全性、实时性能、成本与可更新性之间实现平衡，并增强对新密码算法、后量子密码和持续演进型汽车软件生态的适应能力。

Abstract: Automotive electronic control units (ECUs) increasingly depend on hardware-rooted security to protect software integrity, authenticity, and lifecycle management in the presence of remote and physical threats. Hardware Security Modules (HSMs) have become a key building block in automotive system-on-chips (SoCs), providing isolated cryptographic services, secure key storage, and controlled execution under stringent real-time and cost constraints. This paper presents an architectural analysis of automotive HSMs and examines their role in establishing secure boot and hardware roots of trust. We first survey common HSM integration models used in production ECUs and discuss their flexibility and current automotive use cases. We then introduce realistic threat models to motivate hardware-backed security controls and analyze how HSM design choices influence secure boot chains of trust, secure storage, secure execution, and software signing mechanisms. Key tradeoffs between isolation, performance, updateability, and attack surface are discussed, with optional consideration of side-channel implications. The paper concludes by highlighting open challenges and future directions for scalable and resilient automotive hardware security. Finally, we discuss emerging challenges such as cryptographic agility and post-quantum readiness that are likely to shape the next generation of automotive HSM architectures.

</details>


### [27] [LLMscope: Extracting LLM Assets from Edge AI Chips via Optical Probing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25321)
*Dev Mehta,Lily Dukette,William Folan,Olivia Kochol,Noah Solomon,Shahin Tajik,Fatemeh Ganji*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文指出，边缘端大语言模型加速器在推理过程中会因参数与中间状态反复写入片上存储和计算单元，而面临物理侧信道泄露风险。作者利用激光电压成像，在FPGA大模型加速器上实现了对嵌入、注意力相关数据、量化MLP权重、激活值等资产的恢复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLM推理部署到边缘AI芯片，模型权重和推理中间状态成为高价值资产。现有加速器为提高资源利用率而复用缓冲区与计算电路，这种硬件执行特征可能使攻击者通过局部物理观测重建敏感数据，因此需要量化该风险及其恢复成本。

Method: 作者部署激光电压成像，对FPGA上的LLM加速器进行局部存储器和计算子电路探测。攻击通过在推理时定位不同地址、计算块、模块和层对应的复用缓冲区，读取目标资产的值；对于部分权重或比特无法直接读出的情况，进一步提出基于剩余可观测信息恢复资产的方法，并推导成像工作量与目标资产维度之间的下界关系。

Result: 实验表明，攻击者能够完整恢复所选目标值，包括嵌入、注意力相关状态、量化MLP权重和激活值等。即使存在未读出的权重或比特，所提方法仍可恢复目标资产。理论分析显示，直接恢复的成像成本随目标资产规模线性增长。

Conclusion: 该工作证明了边缘LLM加速器存在现实的物理侧信道资产泄露风险，且硬件资源复用会扩大可观测范围。线性成本意味着大规模资产恢复并非成本恒定，但高价值或局部模型数据仍可能遭受严重泄露；因此，加速器设计需考虑存储隔离、执行随机化、侧信道防护与物理访问防御。

Abstract: The move of LLM inference to edge AI accelerators introduces new physical vulnerabilities. During execution, model parameters and intermediate inference states are repeatedly loaded into and processed on the chip, making them suscep- tible to physical side-channel attacks. In this work, by deploying laser voltage imaging, we show that one can extract LLM assets during inference, namely embeddings, attention, and quantized MLP weights, activations, and other inference states, from localized memories and compute subcircuits. To validate our claims, we perform an attack on an FPGA-based LLM accelerator. Since such accelerators reuse the same buffers and compute subcircuits across addresses, tiles, modules, and layers, reading asset values comes down to probing different memories during inference. We demonstrate full recovery of the targeted values; however, we also establish a methodology to recover asset values even if some weights or bits remain unread. We further derive lower bounds that relate imaging effort to asset dimensions and show that even direct recovery scales linearly with the size of the targeted asset

</details>


### [28] [Here is a GIFT: Enforcing User Data Isolation in LLM Serving via GPU Information Flow Tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25431)
*Jiacheng Shi,Xunjie Wang,Cheng Tan,Jinyu Gu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文提出GIFT，一种面向大语言模型推理服务的GPU信息流跟踪系统。其通过按用户加密与静态分析GPU内核信息流，实现共享CPU/GPU基础设施上的用户数据隔离；扩展方案GIFT-CC结合机密计算，进一步覆盖不可信系统软件环境。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型服务框架通常在共享硬件上处理多用户数据。现有隔离机制若依赖修改CPU侧服务逻辑、插桩GPU内核或同步跟踪，可能带来兼容性问题和较高性能开销。因此，需要一种不妨碍服务框架演进、且能以低开销保障跨用户数据不混合的机制。

Method: GIFT基于两项设计：其一，利用CPU组件主要负责调度和数据搬运、较少直接处理内容的特性，对不同用户数据实施独立加密，以加密状态形成隔离边界，而无需改造CPU侧服务逻辑；其二，针对GPU内核信息流有限且规律明确的特点，预先对每类内核进行静态信息流分析并生成规则。运行时采用解耦式跟踪执行这些规则，避免GPU内核插桩和停顿。GIFT-CC在此基础上接入机密计算能力，以加强对系统软件层威胁的防护。

Result: 作者在vLLM和DistServe上实现并评估了GIFT及GIFT-CC。实验表明，两种方案均可实施用户数据隔离，吞吐量开销为4%至10.7%，同时保持与基线相同的延迟水平。

Conclusion: GIFT将每用户加密与GPU内核静态信息流规则结合，在不侵入CPU侧服务框架的前提下，实现了面向LLM推理服务的低开销数据隔离。其扩展GIFT-CC表明，该思路还可与机密计算协同，以扩大保护范围。

Abstract: LLM serving frameworks process large volumes of user data--often containing sensitive information--on shared infrastructure. Ensuring isolation between users who share the same serving framework (on CPUs) and LLM operators (on GPUs) is critical for privacy protection.
  This paper presents GIFT, a GPU Information Flow Tracking system that enforces user data isolation in LLM serving with minimal overhead. Moreover, the design of GIFT is non-intrusive and allows CPU-side serving frameworks to evolve freely. It rests on two key insights. First, encryption-as-isolation leverages the observation that CPU components only orchestrate data flow, not content manipulation; thus, per-user encryption can provide isolation without modifying serving logic. Second, GPU kernels exhibit limited and predictable information flows, enabling static flow analysis. GIFT precomputes information flow rules for each kernel and uses decoupled flow tracking, avoiding instrumentation or GPU stalls.
  Furthermore, we extend GIFT to GIFT-CC, which integrates confidential computing to protect against untrusted operating systems and hypervisors (LLM service providers). Implemented on vLLM and DistServe, GIFT and GIFT-CC enforce user data isolation with a 4-10.7% throughput overhead while maintaining the same latency level.

</details>


### [29] [MACGen: Toward Functionally Correct and Secure Code Generation via Multi-Agent Collaboration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25457)
*Miseon Yu,Jaehoon Choi,Younghan Lee,Yunheung Paek*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文提出MACGen，一个以结构化工件传递信息的多智能体安全代码生成框架。该方法将功能规划、漏洞分析、代码生成与审查分离，在CWEval和BaxBench上相对直接提示分别平均提升F&S@1 19.61和10.57个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型虽具备代码生成能力，但常难以同时满足功能正确性与安全性。现有检索式方法提供的安全建议通常较通用，仍需生成器自行映射到具体任务；共享完整对话历史的多智能体方案则可能造成职责混淆和上下文冗余。

Method: MACGen采用流水线式角色分工：规划器生成满足功能需求的步骤计划；安全顾问预测潜在CWE类别并形成任务相关的安全指南；编码器依据计划与指南生成代码；审查器从相互区分的视角给出反馈以支持修正。代理间不共享完整对话，而仅传递上游产生的结构化工件，从而强化角色边界并控制上下文规模。

Result: 在CWEval与BaxBench基准上，MACGen的功能与安全联合指标F&S@1相较直接提示平均分别提高19.61个百分点和10.57个百分点，表明分阶段的安全分析与受控信息传递能够提升首次生成代码的综合质量。

Conclusion: 该研究表明，安全代码生成适合被建模为功能与安全的联合优化问题。通过任务化安全指南、明确的代理职责和结构化上下游信息接口，MACGen能够缓解通用建议难落地及多智能体上下文膨胀的问题，并取得显著的综合性能增益。

Abstract: Despite their strong ability to generate code, large language models often fail to produce secure code, as their outputs frequently contain security vulnerabilities. Secure code generation is inherently challenging because it requires solving a multi-objective problem: functional correctness and security. Existing approaches address this challenge by injecting external security knowledge or by using agentic feedback and iterative refinement. However, guideline retrieval often leaves the generator to translate generic advice into task-specific secure implementations, while shared-dialogue multi-agent feedback can blur role boundaries and suffer from context bloat.
  We present MACGen, a multi-agent framework that integrates planning, security analysis, code synthesis and refinement to jointly optimize security and functionality. A planner constructs a step-by-step plan to satisfy functional requirements. A security advisor identifies likely CWEs and synthesizes task-specific guidelines, a coder then generates code grounded in these artifacts, and a reviewer issues perspective-separated feedback. Rather than sharing full dialogue histories, each agent receives only structured artifacts from upstream stages, enforcing role specialization and reducing uncontrolled context growth. On CWEval and BaxBench, MACGen improves F&S@1 over direct prompting by 19.61 and 10.57 percentage points (pp) on average, respectively.

</details>


### [30] [Separating Disclosure from Authorization: Field-Tier Minimization for Agent Action Mediation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25474)
*Jiten Oswal,John Cadeddu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文提出一种面向可审计账本的参数最小化架构：按“字段”而非按“动作类型”分级披露，并在最小化前对完整原始参数计算规范化摘要，使历史承诺独立于后续字段分级调整。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 授权系统需要查看足够信息以执行策略，审计系统需要记录足够证据以验证决定；传统做法容易将收款方、备注、记录标识符等原始参数写入不可删除账本。论文旨在将“可授权性”和“可审计性”分离，减少账本中的敏感或无关信息暴露，同时保持离线可验证性与规则一致性。

Method: 方法将每个参数字段分为三层：可被策略合法直接匹配的字段以原始值传递；与策略相关但具识别性的字段仅以投影形式传递，如邮件域名或模板化路由形状；对策略无合法用途的字段不离开工作负载。系统在最小化前计算完整未裁剪参数的规范化摘要，并将其作为账本承诺。字段分级表、策略模式和传输模式由同一份逐动作声明生成，以避免决策方与记录方规则不一致。论文还按“哪一方能够对某事实无从被察觉地撒谎”分配证明责任：客户端计算参数摘要；客户端不能自行指定所适用定义；客户端声明使用的分级表以检测配置分歧。

Result: 该设计使字段重新分级仅改变披露内容，而不会使既有账本条目失效、无需重新计算哈希，也不会改变离线验证器检查的承诺。统一声明可降低策略与记录规则漂移风险。论文进一步给出各类投影的泄露分析，并报告一个初版投影仍保留原本欲移除标识符的事件，表明投影设计必须接受专门审查。

Conclusion: 核心贡献是以“最小化前完整参数的稳定承诺”解耦审计完整性与数据披露策略，并通过字段级分层和生成式模式一致性实现可演进的最小披露审计。该方案并未消除全部信任假设：客户端对其持有数据及所声明分级表的真实性仍构成残余信任边界，且投影可能产生意外可识别性或关联泄露。

Abstract: A system that authorizes an action must see enough of it to decide, and a system that attests to its decision must record enough to be audited. Both pressures push raw action parameters -- recipients, payment memos, record identifiers -- into an append-only ledger that cannot delete them. We show the two are separable. We classify each parameter field, not each action class, into three tiers: fields a policy may legitimately match on, which cross raw; fields that are policy-relevant but identifying, which cross only as projections such as an email domain or a templated route shape; and fields with no legitimate policy use, which never leave the workload. The central property is that the ledger's commitment is a canonical digest of the full, unminimized parameters, computed before minimization runs. The commitment is therefore independent of the tier table: reclassifying a field changes what is disclosed without invalidating a historical entry, reopening a hash, or altering what an offline verifier checks. Tier table, policy schema and wire schema are generated from one per-action declaration, so the deciding and recording parties cannot hold different rules. We then address a question the architecture forces: which party should compute each attested fact? We argue it is settled by which party could lie about it undetectably, and derive three answers within one request -- the client computes the parameter digest, being the only party holding the data; it is structurally prevented from naming the definition that governed it, since that would write a false statement into a signed ledger; and it attests which tier table it applied, so divergence is detectable. We give a leakage analysis of each projection, report an incident in which a first-cut projection preserved the identifier it was written to remove, and state the residual trust the design does not eliminate.

</details>


### [31] [MMJailBench: A Factorized Benchmark for Disentangling Multimodal Jailbreak Vulnerabilities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25490)
*Tianshi Wang,Jingsong Wang,Yafei Huang,Fengling Li,Xin Li,Lei Zhu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文提出MMJailBench，一个将多模态越狱测试中的关键因素解耦并可控组合的基准，用于定位不同多模态大模型的脆弱性来源，并配套提供可扩展的评测工具链。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评测样例通常同时混合有害意图、提示措辞、视觉语义和指令载体等因素，导致测试结果难以归因，无法判断模型脆弱性究竟由哪一因素驱动，也难以系统比较不同模型的安全对齐缺口。

Method: MMJailBench对四类因素进行因子化设计：有害意图类别、提示框架、视觉语义以及指令呈现载体，并在受控条件下组合生成测试配置。作者在16个开放权重与闭源模型上进行大规模评测；随后针对一个代表性开放模型，分析其内部表征与跨模态交互特征。论文还构建模块化评测套件，支持完整与轻量配置、多种裁判模型及多维度指标。

Result: 不同模型呈现显著异质且依赖模型本身的脆弱性分布；不同风险领域的表现差异明显，说明当前多模态安全对齐覆盖不均。提示框架是导致结果变化的最主要因素；与任务相关的视觉语义会稳定提升越狱成功倾向，其中带有权威暗示的视觉线索尤为突出。相比直接文本指令，图像化呈现的指令并未稳定地增加脆弱性。诊断实验进一步发现了与脆弱性相关的内部表征和跨模态交互模式。

Conclusion: 该工作将多模态越狱评测从整体成功率比较推进到因素级归因，有助于识别模型在特定提示、视觉线索和风险领域中的安全薄弱点。其结论表明，改进多模态安全性应优先关注提示框架鲁棒性、任务相关视觉上下文及权威线索，而不能简单假设图像内指令天然更具风险。

Abstract: Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) are increasingly deployed in real-world applications, yet how different factors shape their jailbreak vulnerabilities remains poorly understood. Existing benchmarks often couple harmful intent, prompt framing, visual semantics, and instruction carrier within individual jailbreak instances, obscuring the specific sources of observed vulnerabilities. To address this limitation, we introduce MMJailBench, a factorized benchmark that systematically varies and combines these factors under controlled configurations, enabling fine-grained comparison and factor-level attribution. Large-scale evaluations across 16 open-weight and proprietary MLLMs reveal highly heterogeneous and model-dependent vulnerability profiles. Jailbreak vulnerability varies markedly across harm domains, exposing uneven coverage in current multimodal safety alignment. Prompt framing emerges as the dominant source of variation, task-relevant visual semantics systematically increase jailbreak susceptibility with authority-like cues exposing particularly pronounced vulnerabilities, and visually rendered instructions do not consistently increase jailbreak susceptibility relative to direct textual instructions. To further investigate the risks introduced by multimodal context, we conduct diagnostic analyses on a representative open-weight model and identify vulnerability-associated patterns in internal representations and cross-modal interactions. Finally, we develop a modular multimodal jailbreak evaluation suite with full and lightweight configurations, multiple judge options, and multidimensional metrics, enabling reproducible, scalable, and cost-efficient multimodal jailbreak auditing.

</details>


### [32] [Defending the Peg: Real-Time Dynamic Protection and Anomaly Detection in DeFi Stablecoins](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25600)
*Hengxing Zeng,Shipeng Ye,Xiaoqi Li*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文面向稳定币智能合约安全，结合典型事件归纳高风险攻击模式，并提出基于链上时序特征与Bi-LSTM的实时异常检测架构；模型在实验中达到96.61%的准确率、97.70%的恶意样本平均召回率，以及1.5—2.8毫秒的单次推理延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稳定币是连接加密资产与传统金融机制的重要基础设施，但其自动化操作依赖部署后难以修改的智能合约。既有研究存在稳定币领域针对性不足、偏静态防护且难适应新型复合风险等问题，因此需要面向真实链上行为的动态、实时防御方案。

Method: 研究首先复盘12起真实安全事件，提炼重入、预言机操纵及复合闪电贷等高风险模式的触发条件与作用机制；随后构建融合多维链上时间序列特征的异常检测模型，并采用双向长短期记忆网络捕获交易行为的前后时序依赖，以支持在线风险分类与预警。

Result: 实验显示，所提Bi-LSTM检测模型分类准确率为96.61%，对恶意攻击样本的平均召回率为97.70%，单次推理耗时为1.5至2.8毫秒。结果表明，该方法在识别覆盖率与实时性之间具有较好的平衡，具备部署于链上交易监测或链下风控服务的潜力。

Conclusion: 论文的主要贡献在于将稳定币场景的事件驱动风险分析与时序深度学习检测结合，形成更具领域适配性的动态防御框架。摘要中的性能指标具有竞争力，但仍需进一步说明数据集规模与标签来源、特征定义、类别分布、与基线方法的对比、跨协议泛化能力，以及在概念漂移和对抗性规避条件下的稳健性。

Abstract: With the rapid evolution of the Decentralized Finance (DeFi) ecosystem, stablecoins have emerged as a critical infrastructure bridging the cryptocurrency market with traditional financial paradigms. However, stablecoin systems rely heavily on smart contracts to execute automated operations. The immutable nature of these systems post-deployment means that the exploitation of security vulnerabilities can lead to irreversible, massive economic losses and potentially trigger systemic financial risks. Current research on stablecoin smart contract security faces challenges such as a lack of domain-specific targeting and the obsolescence of static defense models. To address this, this paper systematically analyzes common attack vectors in stablecoin environments and proposes a practical, real-time dynamic defense architecture. By analyzing 12 real-world security incidents, we elucidate the underlying mechanisms of high-risk patterns such as reentrancy attacks, oracle manipulation, and composite flash loan attacks. Concurrently, we construct a real-time anomaly detection model utilizing multi-dimensional on-chain temporal features and the Bi-LSTM algorithm. Experimental results demonstrate that this model achieves a classification accuracy of 96.61\%, with an average recall rate of 97.70\% for malicious attack samples, and a single inference latency ranging from 1.5 to 2.8 milliseconds.

</details>


### [33] [AI Slop and Hallucinations in Vulnerability Assessment: A Survey on Reasoning Failures and Trustworthy Mitigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25667)
*Junchen Ding,Jialiang Dong,Yichen Zhu,Yi Liu,Gelei Deng,Willy Susilo,Siqi Ma,Yuekang Li*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文是一篇关于大语言模型用于漏洞评估时“AI slop”问题的综述，核心主张是：应以可验证的安全推理取代仅依赖文本流畅性的评估，以降低虚构漏洞、错误补丁和重复改写报告对人工分诊流程的负担。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在安全分诊中可生成表面合理但实际上不成立的漏洞描述、修复建议与报告变体。这类输出会增加人工核验成本、稀释真实信号，并削弱自动化漏洞管理流程的可信度。论文试图统一相关实证现象，解释其成因，并提出可操作的评估与缓解框架。

Method: 论文采用结构化文献综述建立AI slop分类法；将问题根源归因于安全专家的因果演绎推理与自回归概率生成之间的差距；提出“演绎覆盖分数”作为量化代理指标，并讨论链式推理提示和工具调用智能体的改善边界。随后比较检测、水印与主动神经符号验证等方案，最后设计CVE-Bench和Slop-Score两类评测工具，涵盖数据集构建、指标公式及防刷分机制。

Result: 论文认为，链式推理和工具增强能够缩小但无法消除模型生成与安全推理之间的差距；被动检测和水印主要解决来源识别，不能直接保证结论正确性，且受到信息熵等约束。相较之下，结合程序分析、约束求解、测试或形式化验证的主动神经符号方法，更适合验证漏洞结论和补丁有效性。摘要未给出具体实验数值，因此其主要贡献表现为概念框架、指标定义和评测路线，而非新的实证性能结果。

Conclusion: 该综述的价值在于将安全LLM风险从“生成质量”重述为“可证明正确性”问题，并提出以演绎覆盖、基准评测和验证闭环为中心的研究议程。其关键挑战在于：演绎覆盖分数是否能稳定反映真实安全正确性、CVE-Bench与Slop-Score能否覆盖多样化漏洞场景，以及神经符号验证在复杂真实代码库中的成本、可扩展性和误报漏报表现仍需严格实证验证。

Abstract: The integration of Large Language Models (LLMs) into cybersecurity has transformed vulnerability assessment, but it has also produced a trustworthiness crisis driven by the unchecked proliferation of "AI slop." These artifacts, hallucinated vulnerabilities, plausible but incorrect patches, and semantically repackaged bug reports, impose a cognitive burden on human triage pipelines that mirrors a denial-of-service attack. This paper surveys the empirical evidence, identifies a unifying mechanism, and traces a path toward trustworthy triage. We formalize a taxonomy of AI slop grounded in a structured literature review and dissect its root cause: the gap between the causal deductive reasoning of security experts and the autoregressive probabilistic generation of current LLMs. We operationalize this gap through a measurable proxy, the Deductive Coverage Score, and show that chain-of-thought prompting and tool-using agents narrow but do not close it. We review mitigation strategies and argue that passive detection and watermarking target provenance rather than correctness, facing fundamental entropy constraints. We instead advocate for active neuro-symbolic verification, mapping each pipeline component to prior systems with documented limits on security inputs. Finally, we specify two evaluation instruments, CVE-Bench and Slop-Score, including dataset construction, metric formulas, and anti-gaming provisions. By shifting evaluation from linguistic fluency to mathematical verifiability, this survey provides a roadmap for securing emerging AI-driven triage systems.

</details>


### [34] [An Analysis of the Impact of Psychological Factors and Techniques Across Different Types of Social Engineering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25670)
*Helin Omer,Daniela Pöhn*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 研究探讨五类心理因素与五类社会工程攻击类型之间的有效性关系。基于12名参与者对25种刺激的实验结果，鱼叉式钓鱼在权威、信任和贪婪因素下最有效，其中“鱼叉式钓鱼+贪婪”组合整体表现最佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有社会工程攻击类型不断扩展，但不同攻击类型针对特定心理因素的有效性尚不明确。研究旨在识别最有效的攻击类型—心理因素组合，为安全意识培训和防御策略设计提供依据。

Method: 开展实验室研究，共招募12名参与者。每位参与者均接触25种刺激条件，由5类心理因素与5类社会工程攻击类型交叉构成，并比较各组合促使参与者采取目标行为的成功率。

Result: 鱼叉式钓鱼分别在权威、信任和贪婪心理因素下表现为最有效的攻击类型。总体而言，“鱼叉式钓鱼+贪婪”是最成功的组合。弹窗结合权威、短信钓鱼结合权威、语音钓鱼结合好奇心的成功率均为零，属于最不成功的组合。

Conclusion: 不同社会工程攻击类型与心理因素之间存在明显的匹配效应，鱼叉式钓鱼尤其容易利用贪婪、信任和权威心理。不过，样本量仅为12人，且研究属于探索性实验，结论的外部有效性和普适性仍需通过更大规模、更多样化的研究加以验证。

Abstract: Phishing is a well-known social engineering (SE) type used to trick individuals into revealing personal information or performing desired actions, like downloading and installing malware. Other SE types, like vishing and smishing, have emerged and are increasingly being used. As SE continues to successfully persuade victims into actions, the questions arise of which SE attack types are most effective for specific psychological factors (PFs) and, conversely, which PFs are most effective for particular attack types. To answer these questions, we conducted a laboratory study with n=12 participants, in which each participant was shown all 25 stimuli (five PFs and five SE types). The results of this exploratory study show that the most effective SE attack type for authority, trust, and greed was spear-phishing. The most successful combination of PF and attack type was spear-phishing using greed. The least successful combinations were pop-ups using authority, smishing using authority, and vishing using curiosity, each having had no success at all.

</details>


### [35] [LMSM: LLM Security Framework Inspired by Linux Security Modules](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25697)
*XiuYu Zhang,Bonan Ruan,Junfeng Fang,An Zhang,Tat-Seng Chua,Zhenkai Liang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文提出语言模型安全模块（LMSM）框架，将模型内部可解释性信号转化为可部署的运行时安全执行机制，并以模块化架构兼容不同检测后端、策略规则与推理服务系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大模型安全部署虽可利用稀疏自编码器、转码器或探针等内部信号，但通常每种信号均需独立完成校准、策略编写与干预集成，导致工程重复、难以复用，也难以在不改造请求处理链路的情况下更新安全能力。

Method: LMSM借鉴LSM的职责分离思想：安全后端输出经校准的证据；带版本的策略层基于可信的请求上下文评估规则；独立门控层决定缓存生成内容是否释放。框架将“中介执行正确性”与“策略识别有效性”分离，并在Hugging Face Transformers和连续批处理vLLM中实现，支持SAE、转码器及任务拟合的稠密探针，同时处理调度变化下的请求级决策与多规则组合。

Result: 在Qwen3-4B上，LMSM-Checkpoint将HarmBench攻击成功率从39.20%降至3.32%；XSTest误拒率由2.40%升至4.40%。在32条活跃序列条件下，其吞吐量达到无监测匹配服务路径的98.14%，表明安全监测开销较低。

Conclusion: LMSM的核心贡献是建立统一的运行时安全接口，使模型内部分析成果能够以可替换后端、可演化策略和独立执行门控的形式进入生产推理系统。实验显示该架构兼具显著的风险降低效果与较小吞吐损失，但误拒率上升说明实际部署仍需针对不同任务和策略阈值进行校准。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly deployed with layered defenses, yet malicious prompts can still bypass them. Interpretability methods can expose model-internal signals along the generation path that could inform enforcement, but these signals are not security controls by themselves. Deployments that adapt them for safety typically couple each signal to its own calibration, policy logic, and intervention code, so each new artifact creates integration work instead of strengthening a shared defense. We present Language Model Security Modules (LMSM), a security framework that adapts the separation behind Linux Security Modules (LSM) to LLM serving. In LMSM, a selected security backend exposes calibrated evidence, a versioned policy evaluates active rules over trusted per-request context, and a separate gate authorizes buffered output release. This design separates mediation correctness from policy effectiveness, and it allows backend, rule, or schedule changes without rebuilding request handling or enforcement. Our prototype shows the separation working in practice: with Hugging Face Transformers and continuously batched vLLM, the same substrate hosts artifact-backed sparse autoencoder (SAE) and transcoder deployments and task-fitted dense probes, preserves request-specific decisions under scheduler churn, and selectively enforces and composes multiple rules per request. On Qwen3-4B, LMSM-Checkpoint reduces HarmBench attack success rate from 39.20% to 3.32%, with XSTest false refusals rising from 2.40% to 4.40%, while retaining 98.14% of the throughput of a matched serving path that performs no monitoring work at 32 active sequences. LMSM gives advances in interpretability and model-internal analysis a common path to runtime enforcement.

</details>


### [36] [Reassembling Distributed Risk: Trajectory-Conditioned Action Generation for Multi-Turn Agent Safety](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25711)
*Yanbo Dai,Zhenlan Ji,Zongjie Li,Shuai Wang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: ReDiR是一种面向工具调用型大语言模型智能体的生成时安全防御方法，通过压缩并注入跨多轮轨迹的潜在安全证据，在动作生成前识别分散的风险意图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多轮分解攻击可将有害目标隐藏在一系列单独看似合理的请求和工具调用中，使仅检查单步文本或生成后动作的防御难以发现长期累积风险，同时增加推理开销并依赖特定运行时表示。

Method: ReDiR在每次动作生成前，将当前交互轨迹压缩为紧凑的潜在安全表示，并注入冻结的基础模型。该表示通过同模型跨视角监督学习：利用显式任务视角下的安全行为，指导模型从原始多轮轨迹中恢复分散的安全证据，从而在生成过程中直接融合跨轮安全信息。

Result: 在两个智能体安全基准、三类模型架构和八个未参与训练的工具领域上，ReDiR将攻击成功率降低至8%以下，并能迁移到未见工具域；同时保持较高的良性行为保真度，计算开销较低。

Conclusion: ReDiR证明了基于轨迹级潜在安全证据的生成时防御能够有效应对多轮分解风险，在无需独立动作级安全模块的情况下兼顾安全性、跨域迁移能力和效率。

Abstract: Tool-using LLM agents extend security risks beyond generated text to actions that affect external systems. Under multi-turn decomposition attacks, a harmful objective can be distributed across individually plausible requests and tool calls, becoming apparent only from the accumulated trajectory. Existing defenses either rely on auxiliary online reasoning to recover long-horizon security evidence or assess actions after generation, often incurring additional inference cost or depending on runtime-specific action representations.
  We propose \emph{Reassembling Distributed Risk} (ReDiR), a generation-time defense that conditions action generation on trajectory-level security evidence. Before each action, ReDiR compresses the current trajectory into a compact latent safety representation and injects it into the frozen base model. The representation is learned through same-model, cross-view supervision, where safe behavior from an explicit task view provides supervision for recovering distributed safety evidence from the original multi-turn trajectory. This design enables ReDiR to integrate cross-turn security information directly within the generation process without relying on a separate action-level safety module. We evaluate ReDiR on two agent-safety benchmarks across three model families and eight held-out tool domains. ReDiR reduces attack success rates to below 8\%, transfers to unseen tool domains, and preserves benign fidelity with low computational overhead.

</details>


### [37] [From Verdict to Diagnosis: Attributable Security Review of Pull Requests](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25730)
*Zhuo Chen,Boyang Wang,Xiyue Zhang,Xiaoyun Xu,Ahmad-Reza Sadeghi,Stjepan Picek,Lichao Wu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文指出自动化代码审查的“阻止”结果不等于正确识别了使PR不安全的目标缺陷，并将该偏差定义为Verdict-Diagnosis（VD）鸿沟。作者提出MalPR-Bench基准与PRGuard审查器；实验显示，PRGuard在总体拦截数接近CodeRabbit时，对目标漏洞的归因识别和证据验证更强，尤其适用于需跨文件或仓库上下文验证的情形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评测通常仅考察自动审查工具是否阻止含缺陷的PR，但工具可能因无关问题而阻止PR，修复该问题后目标缺陷仍然存在。因此，仅以判定结果衡量会高估工具的实际安全价值。论文旨在建立可验证的归因评测标准，区分“阻止PR”与“准确诊断目标漏洞”。

Method: 作者构建MalPR-Bench，包含44个仓库、8类语言中的89个含目标漏洞PR及50个配对正常PR。每个案例预先定义目标漏洞、可接受的机制说明、必需的仓库证据及不计分的无关发现。评测将结果拆分为判定正确性、目标漏洞识别和证据验证；只有三者均满足才记为可归因拦截。PRGuard通过有界检索及确定性、非执行式工具生成候选漏洞，并核验其前提是否得到仓库证据支持。

Result: 在31个常见覆盖范围的留出缺陷PR上，PRGuard与CodeRabbit的拦截数量相近，分别为22/31和24/31；但PRGuard正确识别目标漏洞22例，CodeRabbit为16例，前者为后者的1.38倍。在14个缺失型案例中，两者均拦截9例，但PRGuard识别9个目标，CodeRabbit仅识别3个。CodeRabbit在所需证据位于改动文件内时识别16/24个目标，而当验证需要改动文件外的证据时为0/7。作者还在5个项目中发现12个先前未披露且具有概念验证支持的问题；在这些发现PR上，两者均拦截10/12，但可归因拦截分别为PRGuard 10/12、CodeRabbit 4/12。

Conclusion: 论文的核心结论是，单独使用“是否阻止PR”作为指标会显著夸大自动化代码审查的安全效益。应采用同时要求正确判定、目标缺陷诊断和仓库证据验证的可归因评测。PRGuard表明，结合机制化候选生成、跨仓库证据核验与受控检索，可在不牺牲总体拦截能力的前提下提升审查结论的可解释性与安全有效性。

Abstract: Automated code reviewers are increasingly used as gates on pull requests (PRs), yet evaluations measure whether they block a malicious change. A block may be triggered by an unrelated issue rather than the vulnerability that makes the PR unsafe; fixing the reported issue can leave the target defect exploitable. We call this discrepancy the Verdict-Diagnosis (VD) gap.
  We present MalPR-Bench, a mechanism-grounded benchmark of 89 malicious PRs and 50 paired benign controls across 44 repositories and eight language families. Each malicious case has a pre-committed rubric specifying the target vulnerability, accepted mechanism descriptions, required repository evidence, and off-target findings receiving no credit. Reviews are scored separately for verdict correctness, target-vulnerability identification, and evidence validation; an attributable block requires all three. We introduce PRGuard, an attributable PR security reviewer that constructs candidate vulnerabilities and validates their premises against repository evidence using deterministic, non-executing tools and bounded retrieval.
  Across 31 common-coverage held-out malicious PRs, PRGuard and CodeRabbit produce similar blocking totals (22/31 vs. 24/31), but PRGuard identifies 22 target vulnerabilities versus 16 for CodeRabbit, a 1.38x difference. On 14 absence-type cases, both block 9, while PRGuard identifies 9 targets versus 3. CodeRabbit identifies 16/24 targets when required evidence lies within touched files and 0/7 when validation requires evidence outside them. Finally, PRGuard uncovers twelve previously undisclosed, proof-of-concept-backed vulnerabilities across five projects. PRGuard/DeepSeek and CodeRabbit both block 10/12 discovery PRs, but produce 10/12 and 4/12 attributable blocks, respectively. Thus, verdict-only evaluation can substantially overstate the security value of automated review.

</details>


### [38] [Pointing the Way, Hiding the Destination: Practical Private Dense Retrieval at Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25735)
*Peichun Hua,Danyang Chen,Junan Zhang,Haifeng Sun,Jingyu Wang,Diwen Xue,Mingyu Li,Yunming Xiao*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文提出一种面向托管式RAG与语义搜索的私密稠密检索方案：以随机化深度哈希生成短候选列表，再结合加密重排序与不经意密钥传输，在保护查询和最终选择的同时避免全语料密码学检索开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 托管检索服务需要同时满足查询及选中结果的隐私保护、以及仅返回用户有权限访问的文档。现有方法通常需对每次查询处理整个语料库，成本过高；或仅扫描少量聚类以提高效率，但可能降低召回与检索质量。

Method: 方法将学习式深度哈希用作私密过滤器：用户查询被映射为带随机性的二进制编码，服务端据此定位较短的候选集；随后通过加密重排序确定候选文档的相关性，并通过不经意密钥传输保护最终文档选择。代码还采用方向度量差分隐私，以抑制嵌入反演和属性推断泄露。

Result: 在五个零样本语料库、规模从2.5万至540万篇文档的实验中，候选集仅需200至500篇即可接近全语料检索质量。在含268万段落的NQ语料库和10-Gbps链路上，该协议相对128-token Qwen3-32B RAG流水线仅增加0.73秒，即约10%的额外延迟；同时显著降低嵌入反演与属性推断风险。

Conclusion: 该工作表明，经精心训练并随机化的短名单可作为高效私密检索的前置过滤层，在基本保持全库稠密检索质量的条件下，显著减少密码学计算与通信负担，使私密RAG检索具备更强的实际可部署性。

Abstract: Hosted retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) and semantic search allow users to query valuable provider-held corpora, raising two competing demands: to hide each query and chosen result, yet reveal only the documents that the user is authorized to receive. Existing cryptographic approaches either make this costly by processing the entire corpus for every query, or sacrifice quality for efficiency by scanning a few clusters. We repurpose learned deep hashing as a private filter: a randomized binary code points the provider to a short candidate list, while encrypted reranking and oblivious key transfer protect the precise query and final selection. This shortlist short-circuits full-corpus cryptographic search without sacrificing retrieval quality: with 200-500 candidates, it closely matches full-corpus retrieval across five zero-shot corpora spanning 25K to 5.4M documents. On the full 2.68M-passage NQ corpus over a 10-Gbps link, our protocol only adds 0.73 seconds, or 10 percent, to a 128-token Qwen3-32B RAG pipeline. The released code satisfies directional metric differential privacy (DP) and substantially reduces embedding-inversion and property-inference leakage, demonstrating that a carefully learned shortlist can make private dense retrieval both accurate and practical.

</details>


### [39] [MeMark: Membrane-Space Watermarking for Spiking Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25738)
*Roberto Riaño,Gorka Abad,Stjepan Picek,Aitor Urbieta*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文提出MeMark，一种面向SNN预训练检查点复用场景的内部状态水印。其将多比特密钥嵌入选定LIF神经元的膜电位状态，而非易被替换的输出头，从而在微调、剪枝、量化及输出头完全替换后仍可验证来源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有SNN水印多依赖模型输出进行验证。当使用者复用预训练骨干并替换任务输出头时，原有验证证据会被移除，难以证明衍生检查点的归属。因此需要一种与输出层解耦、可在骨干复用条件下保留的水印机制。

Method: MeMark选择部分LIF神经元，并为每个神经元分配一个密钥比特。通过秘密输入，使目标神经元的膜电位落在其放电阈值的一侧；验证时以同一阈值直接判定膜电位所在侧来恢复比特。该设计不依赖训练额外解码器。实验覆盖循环、卷积、残差和Transformer类SNN，并考察微调、90%剪枝、int8量化、输出头替换、随机伪造密钥、密钥感知或未知的移除、部分密钥泄露、回滚及学生模型提取等情形。

Result: 在2.154亿参数的SpikeGPT检查点上，20个相互独立的64位真实密钥均满足51/64固定验证阈值；对20个受保护检查点及干净模型进行测试时，30000个新生成随机密钥均未通过。20个真实密钥在微调、90%剪枝、int8量化和输出头替换后仍全部超过阈值。作者报告称，在其设定的威胁模型和测试条件下，自适应移除策略可削弱水印，但未能消除其归属验证证据。

Conclusion: MeMark通过将水印置于LIF神经元内部膜状态，解决了输出头替换导致的传统输出水印失效问题，并在多种SNN架构与常见检查点变换后表现出较高鲁棒性和较低随机误验率。其核心价值在于为预训练SNN骨干及其衍生检查点提供可验证的来源证据；但结论依赖于所声明的攻击模型、密钥保密假设及已测试的模型和变换范围。

Abstract: Spiking Neural Networks (SNNs) are increasingly distributed as pretrained checkpoints and reused as backbones for new tasks. However, current SNN watermarks are mainly verified against the model output. Thus, a user who replaces the output head can keep most of the original network while removing the evidence used for verification. We present MeMark, a watermark designed for the checkpoint-reuse setting. Instead of storing the watermark in the output head, MeMark embeds a multi-bit identifier in the internal membrane state of selected Leaky Integrate-and-Fire (LIF) neurons. A secret input drives each selected neuron to the chosen side of its own firing threshold, and the same threshold is later used to recover the secret bit, so the verifier does not need a learned decoder. We evaluate MeMark across recurrent, convolutional, residual, and transformer SNNs. On a 215.4M-parameter SpikeGPT checkpoint, all 20 independent 64-bit keys pass the fixed 51/64 verification rule, while none of the $30\,000$ fresh random keys pass when tested against all 20 protected checkpoints and the clean model. All 20 genuine keys also remain above the threshold after fine-tuning, 90\% pruning, int8 quantization, and output-head replacement. Under our stated threat model, adaptive attacks can weaken the watermark but do not remove the ownership evidence in the settings we test. Additionally, we study false ownership claims, key-aware and key-agnostic removal, partial key disclosure, rollback, and extraction into a student. The results show that MeMark can provide evidence of checkpoint derivatives, while being resistant to the adversary's attacks and complete head replacement.

</details>


### [40] [Toward Interpretable Privacy Guarantees in Face-Swapping Anonymization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25750)
*Vishnu Bondalakunta,Arman Zareian Jahromi,Shuangqing Wei,George Amariucai*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文研究人脸交换中的目标身份泄漏问题，提出在身份嵌入空间中建模人脸交换变换的线性随机模型，以解释泄漏机制并预测隐私风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管人脸交换通常能使识别系统降低对目标身份的匹配分数，但实证结果显示目标身份信息仍会显著泄漏。现有隐私评估主要依赖观察性指标，缺乏可解释、可预测的理论基础。

Method: 对多种人脸交换模型进行实证分析，并将人脸交换器抽象为身份嵌入空间上的线性随机变换。利用实验观测拟合模型，再据此推导可检验的身份泄漏预测。

Result: 研究发现，不同人脸交换模型普遍存在显著的目标身份泄漏。所提出的模型能够以可解释方式描述泄漏机制，并为隐私风险提供可检验的预测，而不只是依赖经验性识别分数。

Conclusion: 人脸交换不能被简单视为充分的身份隐私保护手段。在线性随机嵌入模型的支持下，隐私评估可以从经验观察推进到具有解释性和可完善性的形式化分析。

Abstract: Face-swapping has emerged as a promising approach to facial privacy protection, replacing a target individual's appearance with that of a donor while preserving non-facial context. The resulting images visually resemble the donor, and face recognition systems tend to suppress the target's match scores -- ostensibly satisfying privacy requirements. Empirical evaluation across a range of face-swapping models, however, reveals that significant target identity leakage still occurs. This raises a deeper question: why does leakage occur, and can it be predicted? We propose a linear stochastic model that treats face-swappers as transformations on the space of identity embeddings, providing an interpretable account of the leakage mechanism. The model is fit to empirical observations and used to derive testable predictions. The aim is to ground privacy assessments in principled, interpretable analysis, thus making formal privacy guarantees explainable -- and perfectible -- rather than purely observational.

</details>


### [41] [EVOMAL: Self-Poisoning in Self-Evolving Coding Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25776)
*Xiaodong Wu,Yu Shi,Qi Li,Zhimin Zhao,Xiangman Li,Bram Adams,Ahmed E. Hassan,Jianbing Ni*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文揭示了自演化大语言模型编码代理在从共享技能库检索并仿写工具时存在“自污染”风险：恶意技能可被代理模仿、重写、存储并执行，进而在技能库中持续复制。作者提出EvoMal验证该风险，并提出counter-prompt缓解方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有技能库防护主要关注外部提交者直接植入的名称、代码或特征签名，却忽略了代理在正常工具创作过程中会将检索内容作为模板重组。论文旨在说明这种“代理自行生成的副本”会绕过传统溯源式防护，并在原始植入样本删除后仍持续存在。

Method: 作者构造EvoMal：将可替换载荷封装在具有良性外观的结构化“横幅”中，以提高代理仿写时保留其中代码的概率。攻击者仅向技能库植入样本而不主动调用；代理在任务执行中检索、创作并运行含有相同载荷的新技能。研究以代理自污染率（ASPR）衡量新增恶意技能的任务比例，在六个模型和153个SWE-bench Verified工具相关任务上测试，并评估定向描述、移除初始样本后的持久性及counter-prompt防御效果。

Result: 六个模型的ASPR为20.3%至41.8%，受污染技能库中的相关技能数量达到初始植入量的4.9至9.0倍。即使不使用横幅，DeepSeek-V4-Pro仅凭载荷也达到11.1% ASPR；将植入技能描述定向匹配某一任务族后，ASPR可达86.7%。删除初始植入样本后，Qwen3在第5轮仍有68%的ASPR，表明代理生成副本可形成持久传播。counter-prompt将EvoMal的ASPR降至最高6.7%，且未观察到显著任务完成率下降。

Conclusion: 论文表明，具备检索、仿写、存储和执行闭环的编码代理存在由模仿机制驱动的自传播污染面。防御不能仅检查外部样本身份或静态特征，还应约束代理对检索代码的结构性复制、追踪技能谱系，并在创作与入库环节实施内容隔离和验证。counter-prompt展示了低成本缓解潜力，但其对非横幅式变体、跨模型泛化及长期自适应对抗的稳健性仍需进一步验证。

Abstract: Self-evolving LLM coding agents write their own tools by imitating retrieved skills from shared skill libraries. We identify a vulnerability in this loop: during authoring, a retrieved malicious skill can become the template for a new skill that preserves the payload. We call this self-poisoning: the agent authors, stores, and runs the resulting malicious skill. We exploit it through EvoMal, an attack that amplifies self-poisoning by wrapping an interchangeable payload in a banner, a set of benign-looking structural elements that induces an imitating agent to reproduce the enclosed code. The attacker plants malicious skills in the library without invoking them. The agent then authors and executes new skills carrying the harmful code. Each authored copy can re-enter the library and be imitated again, forming a self-propagating worm that persists after the planted skills are removed. We define the agent self-poisoning rate (ASPR) as the fraction of tasks that add a newly authored malicious skill to the library. Across six models on 153 tool-relevant SWE-bench Verified tasks, ASPR ranges from 20.3% to 41.8%, and the poisoned libraries hold 4.9 to 9.0 times as many malicious skills as were planted. The vulnerability also appears without a banner: DeepSeek-V4-Pro reaches 11.1% ASPR with the payload alone. Tailoring the planted skill descriptions to one task family raises ASPR to 86.7%. After the planted skills are removed, Qwen3 retains a round-5 ASPR of 68% because agent-authored copies remain. These copies evade existing defenses, which focus on attacker-submitted names, code, and signatures. We propose counter-prompt, a defense that discourages banner-style copying and reduces EvoMal's ASPR to at most 6.7% with no significant task-completion loss.

</details>


### [42] [Closing the Gap: Automated Discovery of Secure Dockerfile Reference Standards via Semantic Clustering in Enterprise Inner Source](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25793)
*Jessica Hösl,Benedikt Hofmann,Patrick Stöckle*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文针对企业内源软件环境中的Dockerfile安全配置错误与技术债务，提出自动化分析流程，并发现问题普遍存在；利用组织内部已有的高质量实现，可显著改善安全态势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Dockerfile安全研究主要基于开源生态，难以直接解释企业内部专有代码、治理机制和业务上下文中的问题。本文旨在量化企业Dockerfile的安全与质量缺陷，并评估内部复用和优化的潜力。

Method: 构建自动化分析流水线，爬取企业GitLab中的代码仓库，使用Hadolint、ShellCheck和Trivy提取安全及质量指标，并结合生命周期数据。随后利用大语言模型生成语义描述，通过HDBSCAN聚类识别功能相近的工作负载，再将各文件与集群内参考实现进行对比，以量化优化差距。研究分析了单一大型工业企业中超过6200个仓库的11470个Dockerfile。摘要称流程包含六个阶段，但明确列出的步骤仅有四项。

Result: 99%的Dockerfile至少存在一项安全配置错误，80.8%违反Dockerfile最佳实践；中位数文件已有838天未被修改。与此同时，83%的功能集群中已存在高质量参考实现。采用这些内部标准预计可使平均安全态势得分提升60.4%，且无需开发新的模板。

Conclusion: 企业内源环境中的Dockerfile技术债务和安全缺陷具有系统性，但组织内部已经存在可复用的解决方案。基于语义聚类和参考实现比较的分析方法，可为上下文感知的自动化推荐系统及企业软件供应链安全治理提供依据。不过，研究仅覆盖一家企业，相关缺陷分布和优化收益能否推广至其他组织仍需多组织研究验证。

Abstract: Containerization dominates enterprise software delivery, yet Dockerfiles that assemble container images frequently harbor security misconfigurations and structural technical debt. This problem is poorly understood in corporate inner-source environments, where proprietary context and isolated governance prevent direct application of open-source findings.
  We present an automated, six-stage pipeline that: (1) crawls an enterprise GitLab instance, (2) enriches each Dockerfile with static security and quality metrics (Hadolint, ShellCheck, Trivy) and lifecycle data, (3) groups functionally identical workloads using LLM-generated semantic descriptions and HDBSCAN, and (4) quantifies the optimization gap against cluster-internal reference implementations.
  Applied to 11,470 Dockerfiles from over 6,200 repositories at a single large industrial company, we find a systemic deficit: 99\% of files contain at least one security misconfiguration, 80.8\% violate Dockerfile best practices, and the median artifact has not been revised for 838~days. Despite this, high-quality reference implementations already exist within 83\% of functional clusters. Adopting these internal standards would increase the average security posture score by 60.4\% without developing any new templates. These findings, grounded in one organization's inner-source ecosystem, provide a data-driven foundation for future automated, context-aware recommender systems targeting enterprise supply-chain security; whether the observed technical-debt distribution and optimization gap generalize to other enterprises remains an open question for future multi-organization study.

</details>


### [43] [SkillShield: Prompt-Space Security Skills for LLM Coding Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25817)
*Xiaodong Wu,Zhimin Zhao,Qi Li,Xiangman Li,Yu Shi,Bram Adams,Jianbing Ni*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文提出SkillShield：一种将离线合成的安全“技能”注入系统提示词的防御方法，用于降低编码智能体在工具调用过程中的有害行为与恶意代码生成风险，且无需运行时分类器或请求路由。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 具备文件编辑和命令执行权限的编码智能体可能将不当请求转化为实际风险。现有方案要么依赖模型权重层面的对齐、对仅API部署者不可用，要么依赖输入过滤或执行边界监控等额外组件，增加系统复杂度与运行时依赖。

Method: SkillShield从已知攻击样本或历史失败轨迹中离线归纳安全策略，并在会话开始时将其作为持续生效的系统提示词注入。论文比较三种固定提示词预算下的配置：覆盖全部威胁类别的all-classes、覆盖相关类别子集的per-bundle，以及每类单独配置的per-class；后三者中per-class作为能力上界参考。

Result: 在RedCode基准的六个大语言模型上，默认all-classes配置将恶意代码生成严重度从3.37降至0.58，执行攻击成功率为43.6%，与需要独立8B分类器的Llama Guard 3的42.7%接近。per-bundle和固定类别的per-class分别将该比率降至36.2%和14.5%。在两类非自适应越狱测试中，该方法在恶意代码生成防护上优于所有基线；在731项正常任务中，平均安全误拒率仅0.14%。

Conclusion: 研究表明，系统提示词中的安全技能可作为一种轻量、部署友好的纵深防御手段，在不增加运行时分类或路由组件的前提下，显著改善编码智能体的安全表现。其局限在于效果受提示词预算、已知攻击覆盖度及面对自适应攻击时的稳健性影响，仍需进一步验证。

Abstract: A coding agent edits files and executes shell commands with its developer's privileges, allowing malicious requests to translate directly into harmful actions or functional malware. Existing defenses have complementary limitations: weight-level alignment is unavailable to API-only deployers, whereas input filters and execution-boundary monitors require auxiliary classification or checking components along the agent's trajectory. We therefore introduce SkillShield, a system-prompt defense that synthesizes security skills offline from known attacks or recorded agent failures. These skills are injected into the system prompt at session start and remain active throughout the tool-use loop. Unlike a reference monitor, they protect the system by defining the security policies the model should follow during execution. Due to the limited system-prompt space, we examine three fixed-budget provisioning scopes: all-classes, with one skill covering all threat classes, per-bundle, with one skill targeting a related subset, and per-class, with one skill dedicated to a single known class and used as the upper-bound reference. None requires runtime request classification or routing. Across six large language models on RedCode, the default all-classes skill reduces malware-generation severity from 3.37 to 0.58 and achieves a 43.6% execution attack success rate, comparable to Llama Guard 3's 42.7% without its separate 8B classifier. The per-bundle and class-fixed per-class settings further reduce this rate to 36.2% and 14.5%, respectively. Under two non-adaptive jailbreak families, SkillShield continues to outperform all baselines on malware generation. Across 731 benign task descriptions, SkillShield yields a mean safety-refusal rate of 0.14%. These results demonstrate the potential of prompt-space security skills to prevent harmful actions and malware generation for LLM coding agents.

</details>


### [44] [A Hybrid Security Framework for Mini-Programs: Visual UI Compliance and Network Risk Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25877)
*Panpan Shen,Lei Xie,Xiaoqi Li*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文提出一套面向微信小程序界面交互合规性与跳转安全性的自动检测方案，结合YOLOv8视觉识别和mitmproxy网络抓包，对按钮尺寸、位置、误触风险及点击后的链接跳转进行检测与记录。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 微信小程序具有免安装、轻量和即用即走等特点，应用规模持续扩大。但部分小程序存在按钮或图标尺寸不规范、广告弹窗与支付入口布局易误触、关闭按钮过小或隐蔽等问题，可能导致用户误点击、非自主支付或跳转至不安全页面。因此，需要一种能够同时评估界面设计规范性和操作跳转安全性的检测工具。

Method: 首先，利用YOLOv8对小程序截图中的按钮、图标及相关界面元素进行目标检测，结合鼠标点击位置提取对应元素的类别、位置和尺寸数据，并依据预设规则识别和记录疑似违规项。其次，使用mitmproxy拦截点击操作触发的网络请求，提取重定向链接及相关请求信息，对其是否处于预定义安全范围内进行分析，并将关键结果展示给检测人员。

Result: 摘要表明，系统能够识别小程序界面中的多类按钮和图标，显示被点击元素及其数据，标记并记录尺寸或位置不符合规范的交互元素；同时能够捕获点击产生的请求并分析跳转链接安全性，为人工审查提供关键网络信息。摘要未给出数据集规模、检测精度、误报率、性能开销或实际案例验证结果。

Conclusion: 该研究构建了视觉检测与网络行为分析相结合的小程序合规检测框架，可用于发现易误触的界面设计和潜在不安全跳转问题。其主要价值在于将前端交互规范审查与点击后的网络请求审查联动；后续应通过明确的合规阈值、权威安全判定规则及量化实验，验证系统的准确性、鲁棒性和实用性。

Abstract: With the continuous development of the WeChat ecosystem, WeChat Mini Programs, due to their advantages of not requiring installation, using little memory, and being ready to use instantly, have seen a surge in user numbers and have now become an indispensable service carrier in mobile internet. However, as Mini Programs rapidly became popular, issues regarding the compliance of their interface interaction design and the safety of operational behavior have become increasingly apparent. Many Mini Programs have problems such as clickable buttons and icons not being standard in size, or ad pop-ups and payment entrances being placed in a way that is easy to misclick. The close or cancel buttons are often too small or hidden, making it easy to accidentally click on ads or payment content, and difficult to accurately click the cancel button. This can result in involuntary payments or being redirected to illegal pages, causing unnecessary financial losses and seriously harming users' property security and legal rights. To address the above issues, this article develops a detection program to check the position and size of various icons and buttons in Mini Programs, and analyze whether redirected links fall within a safe range. YOLOv8 is used to identify various buttons in images, displaying the corresponding icon and its data based on the mouse click position. Violations are flagged and recorded. At the same time, mitmproxy is used to capture relevant data requests generated during clicks, analyzing the safety of redirections, and presenting key information for user observation.

</details>


### [45] [A Self-Evolving Multi-Agent Framework Defense against LLM Jailbreak Attacks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26008)
*Tongyan Hu,Bryan Hooi*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文提出一种面向大语言模型越狱提示的测试时自适应防御：将成功攻击归纳为可复用的“方法级规则”存入跨交互持久记忆，并在后续输入中通过提示注入这些规则实现防御，无需参数更新。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有越狱防御通常在部署后保持静态，难以从新出现的攻击包装方式中积累经验。攻击者可通过角色扮演、混淆、代码变换和多步间接等策略持续改变输入形式，因此需要能够在线扩展防御知识、同时避免按具体主题过拟合的机制。

Method: 框架在攻击绕过防御后，将失败案例抽象为描述攻击结构或包装方式的规则，而非记录具体请求主题；规则被持久化保存并用于审查后续交互。该方案以外部记忆和提示工程实现，可适用于开源权重模型及黑盒接口模型。系统包含四个协作模块，但核心贡献是基于规则记忆的测试时适应机制，以及随新型攻击包装出现而扩展的标签空间。

Result: 在四类黑盒越狱攻击族和多个模型上的实验表明，该方法能显著降低攻击成功率，并较好保持正常请求效用；面对自适应的复合包装攻击仍具有鲁棒性。随着规则记忆增加，系统未表现出过度拒答上升。

Conclusion: 方法级、跨交互的规则记忆为静态安全策略提供了无需训练的持续适应能力。其关键优势在于：单条规则可覆盖同一攻击家族的多种实例，且不会因绑定具体敏感主题而明显损害正常使用；论文结论仍依赖规则归纳质量、攻击成功检测的可靠性及对更广泛真实攻击分布的验证。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) remain vulnerable to jailbreak attacks that exploit techniques such as role-playing, obfuscation, code transformation, and multi-step indirection to elicit harmful outputs. As jailbreak strategies keep emerging, defenses have proliferated in an ongoing cat-and-mouse game, yet most remain static: their safety behavior is fixed at deployment, so they cannot accumulate defensive experience or adapt to unseen strategies. We propose a self-evolving test-time defense built around a persistent, cross-interaction rule memory: when an attack succeeds, the framework abstracts that failure into a method-level rule capturing the structural attack wrapper rather than the harmful topic, and reuses it against future inputs. Because rules are method-level, one induced rule generalizes across an entire attack family, and the label space expands as novel wrappers appear. The mechanism operates entirely through external memory and prompting, with no parameter updates, and applies to both open-weight and black-box API models. We realize it as four cooperating modules, but the contribution is the memory-based adaptation mechanism, not the module decomposition. Across four black-box jailbreak families and multiple models, our method substantially reduces attack success rates while preserving benign utility, remains robust under an adaptive composite-wrapper attack, and does not increase over-refusal as the memory grows.

</details>


### [46] [RTLGuard: A Lightweight Teacher-Student Defense for Poisoned RTL Code Generation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26049)
*Mahshid Rezakhani,Kimia Azar,Hadi Kamali*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文提出RTLGuard，通过教师—学生框架净化可能被植入后门的RTL代码生成大语言模型，在降低攻击触发成功率的同时保持生成代码的功能正确性与可综合性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预训练或第三方微调的模型其训练数据与适配过程通常不透明，可能在微调阶段被植入隐藏触发行为。此类风险会使表面正常的输入诱导模型生成存在安全隐患的硬件描述代码，因此需要一种无需全参数重新训练的高效模型净化方案。

Method: RTLGuard先使用少量可信RTL数据微调小规模教师模型，再以复合教师—学生训练目标引导受污染的目标模型进行修正。该过程结合特征对齐与知识蒸馏，使目标模型的内部表示和输出行为向可信教师靠拢，从而抑制异常触发行为，并避免全参数重训带来的高计算成本。

Result: 在多种LLM架构上的实验表明，RTLGuard能够显著降低攻击成功率，同时维持生成RTL代码的功能正确性及综合可实现性。摘要未提供具体数据集、攻击设置、模型规模和量化指标，因此不同架构之间的绝对效果尚无法进一步判断。

Conclusion: RTLGuard为AI辅助芯片设计供应链中的模型可信问题提供了轻量化缓解思路：利用有限可信数据和教师—学生净化训练，可在较低再训练开销下削弱模型中的隐藏恶意行为，并尽量保留RTL生成能力。

Abstract: The rapid advancement of large language models (LLMs) is driving a shift toward automated register transfer level (RTL) code generation, enabling designers to translate high-level specs. into synthesizable hardware. However, this reliance on pre-trained (3rd-party) fine-tuned models may introduce critical trust issues, as the training data and adaptation process of these models are often opaque. Thus, adversaries (even model providers) may embed hidden backdoor threats during fine-tuning, allowing malicious behavior, e.g., hardware Trojans, to be triggered by seemingly benign prompts given by victim user at inference time. In this paper, we introduce RTLGuard, to mitigate such a trust issue in AI-enabled IC supply chain. Rather than prohibitive computational cost of full-parameter retraining, RTLGuard leverages a teacher-student framework designed to sanitize compromised RTL generation models by (1) fine-tuning a small-scale, "clean" teacher model on a limited set of trusted RTL data, (2) guiding the poisoned target model via a composite teacher-student objective, and (3) incorporating feature alignment and knowledge distillation to suppress malicious behaviors. Our experiments across various LLM architectures demonstrate that RTLGuard significantly reduces the Attack Success Rate (ASR) while preserving the functional correctness and synthesizability of the generated RTL code.

</details>


### [47] [From Fleet to Lab: Revisiting the Security and Complexity of Industrial Rowhammer Mitigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26072)
*Hritvik Taneja,Moinuddin Qureshi*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文指出微软 Sigries 的“跟踪—采样”双模式 Rowhammer 缓解机制在模式切换时存在安全缺陷，并提出 FiRM，通过协同设计过滤、采样与缓解策略，在降低存储和硬件复杂度的同时提升安全性与性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方案存在核心权衡：精确跟踪器可减少不必要的缓解操作，但依赖容量较大的 CAM；采样式方案几乎无需跟踪存储，但会引入较频繁的缓解。Sigries 虽结合了欠配 Misra-Gries 跟踪与采样回退，但论文发现其不仅在跟踪转采样时不安全，反向切换同样可被利用，且 CAM 实现和存储开销较高。

Method: 作者首先分析 Sigries 在模式转换期间的状态与覆盖缺口，构造跨子银行的轮转访问模式，证明其反向转换并非始终安全。随后提出 FiRM，以 SRAM 过滤器替代 CAM 跟踪器，并将跟踪模式与采样模式作为统一机制协同配置。FiRM-P 在转换期和稳态采用不同的概率参数；FiRM-D 则通过调节缓解速率提供确定性保障。

Result: 分析表明，Sigries 在所构造的跨子银行轮转模式下，其平均失效时间可降至约 1 秒，较 PARA 所述约 13 年的水平低约 8 个数量级。FiRM 对未超过过滤阈值的正常负载不产生性能减速；FiRM-P 兼顾概率安全性与较低性能开销，FiRM-D 提供确定性安全保证。两种 FiRM 变体的存储开销均低于 Sigries。

Conclusion: Rowhammer 缓解机制不能仅分别保证跟踪模式和采样模式的安全性，还必须显式保证模式转换过程的安全性。FiRM 通过基于 SRAM 的过滤和转换感知的缓解策略，避免了 Sigries 的转换漏洞、CAM 复杂性及较高存储成本，并在安全性、性能和实现代价之间提供了更合理的设计。

Abstract: This paper studies efficient and secure Rowhammer mitigation at the Memory-Controller (MC). Rowhammer mitigation faces a fundamental tradeoff between tracking storage and mitigation rate: precise trackers (such as Misra-Gries) avoid unnecessary mitigations but require large CAM structures, whereas sampling-based schemes (such as PARA) require no storage but incur frequent mitigations even when not under attack. Microsoft recently deployed Sigries, an MC-side Rowhammer defense that combines an under-provisioned Misra-Gries tracker with a row-sampling fallback, in its Azure Cobalt 200 SoC. Sigries observed that the tracker-to-sampling transition can be insecure, and claimed the reverse transition is always safe. Our analysis shows that this transition is also vulnerable, and a Round-Robin Attack across sub-banks reduces the MTTF of Sigries to about 1 second, 8 orders of magnitude below the 13 years with PARA. Sigries also suffers from CAM complexity and high storage overheads.
  Our proposal, FiRM (Filtered Rowhammer Mitigation), is based on the insight that, for a secure design, the tracking-mode and sampling-mode should not be configured independently but co-designed to ensure the system remains secure not only in both modes but also during transitions. FiRM incurs zero slowdown for benign workloads, since they do not exceed the filtering threshold, and also replaces the complex CAM-based tracker with simple SRAM filters. To handle stressful patterns, we propose FiRM-P (probabilistic) and FiRM-D (deterministic). FiRM-P uses varying probabilities during transitions and steady state to ensure both security and low performance overhead. FiRM-D provides guaranteed deterministic security by modulating the rate of mitigation. Both FiRM-P and FiRM-D have less storage overhead than Sigries. Our paper shows that a principled approach can avoid both the insecurity and the complexity of Sigries.

</details>

### [48] [Exact SAT and Constraint Programming for Job Shop Scheduling with Time-Varying Peak Power Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25351)
*Huy Tuan Nguyen,Duc Trung Kim Nguyen,Khanh To Van*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 论文针对带功率约束的作业车间调度问题（JSPPR）提出首个精确SAT与约束规划（CP）模型。在35个公开基准实例上，两类方法均获得并证明了全部实例的全局最优解，显著超过既有MILP和GRASP×ELS结果；其中CP整体快于SAT。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: JSPPR除工序顺序与机器资源约束外，还要求任意时刻的瞬时功率不超过时变上限。既有研究主要采用MILP及GRASP×ELS启发式方法，前者最优性证明覆盖有限，后者不能提供严格最优性证书，且已报告结果存在不一致。因此需要可完整验证最优性的替代精确求解框架与可靠基准。

Method: 作者构建SAT编码和CP模型，将工序加工、机器互斥、工艺先后关系以及时变功率容量约束统一建模；随后在35个已发表基准实例上进行精确求解，并将所得结果与既有MILP、CPLEX、Gurobi及GRASP×ELS报告值进行比较和核验。

Result: SAT和CP均对35个实例证明全局最优，且得到完全一致的最优最大完工时间。CP的最优性证明速度优于SAT。相比之下，CPLEX和Gurobi对应的MILP模型仅分别证明6个和10个实例最优。新结果还将原GRASP×ELS结果中的4个最大完工时间进一步改进，并发现部分既有基准报告值低于已认证的最优值，表明其结果或验证过程存在问题。

Conclusion: SAT与尤其CP是求解JSPPR的有效精确方法，能够实现该基准集的完整最优性认证。论文给出了经验证的最优最大完工时间并纠正既有不一致结果，为后续算法比较、模型评估和基准研究提供了更可靠的标准参考。

Abstract: The Job Shop Scheduling Problem with Power Requirements (JSPPR) extends the classical job shop scheduling problem by imposing time-varying limits on instantaneous power consumption. Previous studies have used a mixed-integer linear programming formulation and the GRASP x ELS metaheuristic, but no SAT-based exact approach or constraint programming model has been reported. This paper develops the first exact SAT and constraint programming (CP) formulations for the JSPPR. On the 35 published benchmark instances, both SAT and CP prove global optimality for all instances and obtain identical optimal makespans, substantially improving upon the best previously reported results. They also establish four improved makespan values over the GRASP x ELS results reported in the original study. CP proves optimality faster than SAT, while both exact approaches substantially improve the optimality coverage of the MILP formulations, which prove optimality on only 6 and 10 instances using CPLEX and Gurobi, respectively. The certified optimal solutions also reveal inconsistencies in several previously reported benchmark results, including makespans below the proven optimum. We provide corrected optimal makespans and a complete set of certified optimal results for the JSPPR benchmark, establishing a reliable reference for future studies.

</details>


### [49] [Dynamic Polyhedral Logic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25691)
*Nick Bezhanishvili,Laura Bussi,Vincenzo Ciancia,David Fernández-Duque,David Gabelaia*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 论文提出时空多面体可达性逻辑，将动态拓扑逻辑扩展到多面体语义，并引入基于路径的空间可达性算子；核心贡献是在可逆动力系统的预期模型类上建立相应逻辑系统的可靠性与完备性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有动态拓扑逻辑能够描述时间演化与空间性质，但对由多面体构成的几何空间及区域间路径可达性的表达能力有限。论文旨在统一描述多面体空间中的内部结构、区域可达性以及由可逆连续变换驱动的时间演化。

Method: 作者以多面体及其多面体子集作为模型与容许赋值，空间模态解释为拓扑内部；二元算子γ(φ,ψ)表示可经由满足φ的区域到达某个满足ψ的点。时间模态由分段线性同胚及其逆映射解释。在此基础上定义h-动态可达空间，并分别为拓扑、有限、Alexandroff和多面体空间上已有可达性逻辑构造对应的h-动态扩展及公理系统。

Result: 论文证明所构建的h-动态扩展逻辑对于目标可逆动力系统类别是可靠且完备的，即可证公式恰好是在相应语义模型中恒真的公式。该结果覆盖多个空间语义层次，并将路径可达性与双向时间演化纳入同一形式化框架。

Conclusion: 该工作为多面体几何环境中的时空推理提供了严格的逻辑基础。其主要价值在于将空间路径可达性、拓扑内部算子和可逆时间动态统一，并通过可靠性与完备性保证公理化系统与预期语义精确对应。

Abstract: We introduce spatio-temporal polyhedral reachability logics, extending dynamic topological logic with polyhedral semantics and a path-based spatial reachability operator. Formulas are interpreted over polyhedra, with admissible valuations ranging over polyhedral subsets; the spatial modality is interpreted as interior, the binary operator $γ(\varphi,ψ)$ expresses reachability of a $ψ$-point through a $\varphi$-region, and the temporal modalities are interpreted by a PL-homeomorphism and its inverse. We define h-dynamic reachability spaces and axiomatize the corresponding h-dynamic extensions of the known reachability logics of topological, finite, Alexandroff, and polyhedral spaces. The main result is soundness and completeness for the intended classes of invertible dynamical systems.

</details>

### [50] [A Programming Paradigm for Spatiotemporal Composability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25512)
*Yifan Shi,Wei Zhang,Tianyi Cui*

Main category: cs.PL

TL;DR: 论文提出“上下文范式”（context paradigm），为动态软件组合建立统一形式基础：以可逆效应保障组件移除时可完整撤销其影响，以反应式余效应管理组件间依赖与生命周期，并通过统一上下文和演算将单组件性质推广至多组件交错运行的系统；作者还实现了名为 Cordis 的元框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 插件系统、动态模块装载和自适应软件需要在运行时加入、移除及重组组件，但现有基础难以同时保证两类关键性质：时间可组合性，即组件卸载后其副作用能够彻底回退；空间可组合性，即组件能够声明依赖并随上下文变化自动激活或失活。论文旨在为这两类问题提供统一、可证明且可实现的机制。

Method: 作者将经典 effect 与 coeffect 概念提升为运行时机制。其一，可逆效应要求每一次上下文变换都附带逆变换，并由运行时保存，以便撤销组件造成的局部影响。其二，反应式余效应将上下文变化与组件的余效应规范进行分类，据此驱动组件激活和停用。随后，论文把效应上下文与余效应上下文合并为单一上下文类型，要求所有效应和余效应均经由该上下文中介；这种中介定义出一种观察等价关系，使不同组件的效应可交错执行而互不干扰。最后，作者定义动态组合组件演算，并给出相应元理论；工程上实现 Cordis，包含效应跟踪、余效应解析、声明式组件加载、配置协调及热模块替换。

Result: 论文声称获得三层结果：第一，在单组件层面，可逆效应建立局部时间可组合性，反应式余效应建立局部空间可组合性；第二，统一上下文及其诱导的观察等价性支持不同组件效应的安全交错；第三，组件演算的元理论将时空可组合性从单个组件推广到由多个交错组件构成的整体系统。Cordis 则表明该理论可落实为面向实际动态装载场景的框架能力。摘要未提供性能数据、形式定理细节或实验比较。

Conclusion: 该工作将“可撤销的运行时影响”和“依赖驱动的反应式生命周期”视为动态组合中相互正交但必须协同的维度，并以统一上下文作为协调边界。其主要贡献在于提出从理论机制、组合演算到框架实现的一体化方案；其价值取决于完整论文中对逆操作约束、观察等价定义、并发或异常情形，以及 Cordis 实际开销和适用范围的具体论证。

Abstract: Modern software -- from plugin systems to self-evolving agent harnesses -- increasingly requires dynamic composition, yet its formal foundations remain underdeveloped. We identify two orthogonal dimensions of the problem: temporal composability, the ability to completely revert a component's side effects upon removal, and spatial composability, the ability to declare and reactively manage inter-component dependencies. We address the two dimensions by lifting classical effect and coeffect concepts to runtime mechanisms. In particular, we formalize revertible effects, in which every context transformation carries an inverse that the runtime holds, establishing temporal composability local to one component. We formalize reactive coeffects, in which every context change is classified against a component's coeffect specification to drive its activation and deactivation, establishing spatial composability local to one component. We then unify the effect context and the coeffect context into a single context type and mediate every effect and coeffect through it, yielding a discipline we call the context paradigm; the mediation induces an observational equivalence up to which the effects of distinct components interleave without disturbing one another. Combining these mechanisms into the notion of a component, we give a calculus of dynamic composition whose metatheory carries spatiotemporal composability from a single component to a whole system of interleaved components. We implement these ideas in Cordis, a meta-framework of spatiotemporal composability that provides a core library with effect tracking and coeffect resolution, as well as a declarative component loader with configuration reconciliation and hot module replacement.

</details>
